(p. 1) śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ | śrīsarasvatyai namaḥ | śrīgurubhyo namaḥ | śrīnāthāya namaḥ | oṃ namo 
brahmādibhyo brahmavidyāsampradāyakartṛbhyo vaṃśa ṛṣibhyo namo gurubhyaḥ |

āgajānana padmārkaṃ gajānanamaharniśam |
anekadantaṃ bhaktānāmekadantamupāsmahe ||

guruṃ gaṇapatiṃ vāṇīṃ sāmbaṃ kṣetrādhipaṃ param |
praṇamya saṃvinmārgasthāna vakṣye svacchandapaddhatim ||

amṛtānandayogīndra bodhād vigata kalmaṣaḥ |
brahmānandākhyayogīndaścacāra pṛthivīmimām ||

vicārya svamatābhijñaṃ vimalānandasaṃjñakam |
svaśiṣyamakarot tasmāt cidānando vabhūva ha? ||

cidānandabodhādakhaṇḍāvabodhaḥ svarūpaṃ viditvā carāmpadvayoham |
tathā śādhimāṃ nātha yuṣmad guruṇāṃ kṛpā tvayyakhaṇḍā bhavat tena śīghram ||

(p. 2) sadānanda sudhārāśi kulālākhyāyaṇam? |
cidānandaguruṃ vande ttvadājñāṃ śirasā vahan ||

kuladharmasya śāstrāṇi vicārya parayā dhiyā |
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya vakṣye svacchandapaddhatim ||

      tatra śrīmān sādhakendraḥ prātaḥ sandhyāpūrvadaṃ udvayātmike ravisturīyayāme brāhme 
muhūrte cotthāya śuddhaḥ san svaśirasi śvetasahasradalakamalakarṇikāyāṃ nijagurun dhyāyet | 
śrīmadbodhaghanāmṛtānvitamahācittena tadvigrahaṃ dhyātvā nityamahaṃ sahasradalakeciccandrabimbaprabham 
| nītvā tadgurumeva ṣoḍaśadale mantrātmakaṃ bhāvayan dhyāyan hṛtkamalodare tu tadahaṃ tvevaṃ 
smare cchrīgurum |
	
      tato mṛgīmudrayā pādukāmantreṇa svaguruṃ trivāraṃ sampūjya svanāma mūlādhāre sakṛt 
sampūjya guruṃ pañcopacāraiḥ pūjayet | tadyathā

      laṃ pṛthivyātmakaṃ gandhaṃ samarpayāmi, haṃ ākāśātmakaṃ puṣpaṃ samarpayāmi, yaṃ 
vāyavyātmakaṃ dhūpaṃ samarpayāmi, raṃ agnyātmakaṃ dīpaṃ samarpayāmi taṃ amṛtātmakaṃ 
naivedyaṃ samarpayāmi yaṃ sarvātmakaṃ tāmbulaṃ samarpayāmi, iti saṃpūjya |

(p. 3) ajñānatimirāndhasya jñānāñjanaśalākayā |
cakṣūnmīlitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ ||

namostu gurave tasmai iṣṭadevasvarūpiṇe |
yasya vāmamṛtaṃ hanti viṣaṃ saṃsārasaṃjñitam ||

ityābhyāṃ yonimudrayā praṇamya svaikyenaiva śivadvaitaguruṃ dhyāyan gurustotramudīrayet -

namaste nātha bhagavan śivāya gururūpiṇe |
vidyāvatārasaṃsiddhyai svīkṛtāneka vigraha ||

navāya navarūpāya paramārthaikarūpiṇe |
sārvājñānatamobhedabhānave cidghanāyate ||

svatantrāya dayāklatpra vigrahāya śivātmane |
paratantrāya bhaktānāṃ bhavyānāṃ bhavyarūpiṇe ||

(p. 4) vivekīnāṃ vivekāya vimarśāya vimarśinām |
prakāśīnāṃ prakāśāya jñānināṃ jñānarūpiṇe ||

purastāt pārśvayoḥ pṛṣṭhe namaskuryāmuparyadhaḥ |
sadā maccittarūpeṇa vidhehi bhavadāsanam ||

tatastaccaraṇāravindamakarandaniṣyandadhārābhiḥ svātmadehamabhiṣiktaṃ vibhāvya pādukāmantraṃ 
sakṛjjapitvā kṣamasveti visṛjediti gurormānasapūjā |

	atha mūlena prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā yogadhyānaṃ kuryāt | mūlakandāddaṇḍalagnāṃ 
brahmanāḍīṃ śvetavarṇāṃ brahmarandhraparyantaṃ gatāṃ smaret | tasya madhye * * * * * * nibhāṃ(?) 
* * * * prabhādīptāṃ tāmūrdhvagāminī mūrti manasā śivātmaka pūrṇacandraṃ nayet |

      mūlā lavāla kuharāduditā bhavānī nirbhidya ṣaṭsarasijāni taḍillateva |
bhūyopi tatra viśati dhruvamaṇḍalendu niṣyandamānaparamāmṛtavṛṣṭirūpā ||

prakāśamānāṃ prathame prayāṇe pratiprayāṇe tvamṛtāyamānām |
antaḥpadatyāganusañcarantīmānandamudrāmavalokayāmaḥ ||

mūlavidyāṃ trikūṭasthāṃ samāhitamanāściram |
mūlādibrahmarandhrāntaṃ visatantu tanīyasī ||

udyadādityarucirāṃ svatanau paricintayet |

iti dhyātvā nyaset -

(p. 68) 	oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ aṃ āṃ iṃ īṃ uṃ ūṃ ṛṃ ṝṃ ḷṃ ḹṃ eṃ aiṃ oṃ auṃ aṃ aḥ 
sūryāya reṇukāmbāyai namaḥ - hṛdayādadhaḥ, 
4 yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ candrāya amṛtāmbāyai namaḥ - bhrūmadhye,

sphuraccandrakalārājata mukuṭāṃ locanatrayām || 12 |

ikṣukodaṇḍapuṣpeṣu pāśāṅkuśacatubhujām ||13 ||

sarvadevamayīmambāṃ sarvasaubhāgyasundarīm |
sarvatīrthamayīṃ divyāṃ sarvakāmaprapūriṇīm || 14 ||

sarvakṣetramayīṃ devīṃ sarvāyatanaśobhatām |
sarvānandamayīṃ jñānagahvarāṃ siddhidāṃ parām || 15 ||

raktapuṣpaniviṣṭāṃ tu raktābharaṇaśobhitām |
brahmaviṣṇuśiroratnanīrājitapadāmbujām || 16 ||

evaṃ dhyāyanmahādevīṃ kadambavanavāsinīm |
kāmeśvaraśivasyāṅke sthāpayet parameśvarīm || 17 ||

vidyayāhvānarūpiṇyā namaskārayutāṃ priye |
gandhapuṣpākṣatādīni devyai samyak nivedayet || 18 ||

(p. 92) upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ sampūjya paradevatām |

iti dhyātvā pañcadaśamudrāḥ pradarśayet |

śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ |

ūrdhvāmnāyaṃ prakūrvīta brahmādīnāṃ ca durlabhām | 
śrīprāsādaparānyāsaṃ parāprāsādameva ca ||

tataḥ paraṃ mahāśaktinyāsaṃ kuryāt | evaṃ tīrthasnānaṃ vidhāya śaktinyāsaṃ kuryāt | 

atha mahāśaktinyāsaḥ -

	asya śrīmahāśaktinyāsamantrasya ādiprakṛtiṛṣiḥ, anuṣṭupachandaḥ, śrī-ādiśaktirdevatā, praṇavo 
bījam, ajapāśaktiḥ, nādakīlakam, mama śrīgurudevaprasādasidhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ |

tāratrayeṇa prāṇāyāmaṃ aṅgulyādiṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ kṛtvā | taduktaṃ śaktikulārṇave -

śrīdevyuvāca

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi śaktinyāsaṃ sureśvara |
e(ye)na dehe niyuktena śaktitulya bhavennaraḥ || 1 ||

e(ye) * * *? nyāsamātreṇa divyadeha prajāyate |
kupitaḥ kurute yogi trailokyasyāpi pātanam || 2 ||

(p. 93) śaktinyāsayuto vīro namaskāreṇa śaṅkara |
sphoṭanaṃ kurute sākṣāt pāṣāṇapratimādiṣu || 3 ||

trikoṇaṃ hastamātraṃ tu śaktinyāsarāga bhavet |
raktacandena kāṣṭhena nirmitaṃ vidhipūrvakam || 4 ||

caturasraṃ ca ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tasyopari trikoṇakam |
śaktinyāsāsanaṃ proktaṃ raktacandananirmitam || 5 ||

ālābhe candanasyāpi kartavyaṃ tilakena tu |
no cetkṣaumādibhiḥ kāryaṃ śaktinyāse mahāsanam || 6 ||

jayā ca vijayā caiva jayantī cāparājitā |
ajitā saṅgamārambhā etāḥ santu mamāsane || 7 ||

saptadhā pūjayed dehe raktapuṣpaiḥ svamastakam |
ātmīyaṃ karabīraiśca no cetkausumbhakesaraiḥ || 8 ||

pūjako mastakepyevaṃ kṛtvā vigrahadhūpanam |
atha tatra niviṣṭastu mantrī dhyānaparo bhavet || 9 ||

(p. 94) uttarābhimukho mantrī raktāmbaravibhūṣaṇaḥ |
raktacandanakarpūre tathā sindūrakuṅkumaiḥ || 10 ||

liptāṅgo raktapuṣpaiśca trimālālaṅkṛtaḥ sudhīḥ |
kaṇṭhadeśe mahādeva tathā rudreṇa bhāṣitam || 11 ||

athātaḥ saṃsthito yogī jāyate śaṅkaropamaḥ |
dvādaśānte cidākāśe śivaśaktyātmakaṃ gurum || 12 ||

paraṃ tejomayaṃ dhyāyed bhogamokṣasukhāptaye |
naguroḥ sadṛśastrātā na devaḥ śaṅkarātparaḥ || 13 ||

na kaulāt paramo yogo na vidyā traipurī samā |
na dīkṣātaḥ paraṃ jñānaṃ na śānteḥ paramaṃ sukham || 14 ||

na śakteḥ ca samaṃ nyāsaṃ na vidyā ṣoḍaśī samā |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi śaktinyāsaṃ sureśvara || 15 ||

śaktinyāsastathā mantra nāmabījākṣarairbhavet |
pūrvabījākṣarairnyāso nāmabhistadanantaram || 16 ||

(p. 95) nāmasyāt pallavasthāne bījasthāne tu bījakam |
bījapallavato loke phalāvāptiḥ pradṛśyate || 17 ||

tathāhi - aiṃ bhagavatī trikoṇāya namaḥ ||

ityādhāre vinyasya yaunau hrāṃ hrīṃ hruṃ nābhimaṇḍale |
klāṃ klīṃ kluṃ bhujayornyasya lhāṃ lhīṃ lhuṃ caraṇe nyaset ||

kapolayośca sāṃ sīṃ sūṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hruṃ karṇayordvayoḥ |
drāṃ drīṃ drūṃ netrayornyasya lāṃ līṃ luṃ nāsikadvayoḥ ||

sāṃ sīṃ sūṃ cubukaṃ nyasya tato mūrdhni pravinyaset |
akārabījaṃ kuru haṃsamaṇḍale samastavarṇān kuru deha jīvite |
te deva līnaṃ kuru yatra vīkṣate tāṃ viddhi sā kāmakalā ṣaḍāśrayā ||

yathā dugdhe sthitaṃ sarpiryathā tailaṃ tileṣu ca |
tathā vargeṣu sarveṣu akāraḥ parikīrtyate ||

akāraḥ kathyate loke mātṛkāṃ viśvamātṛkām |
akārādi kṣakārāntaṃ yo jānāti sa deśikaḥ ||

(p. 96) vāṅmāyā kamalāpūrva sarvamantrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |

oṃ kāra kuṇḍalī vaktre kuṇḍalyapi mahākule |
līyamānā prakartavyā varṇoccāreṇa śaṅkara || 1 ||

līyamānā vijānī hi nirālambe mahāpade |
aprameye virupākṣa kakāre kuṇḍalīṃ kuru || 2 ||

ādikṣāntaṃ samuccārya praṇavaṃ cāntare nyaset |
evaṃ vinyasya bījāni rakṣa rakṣatu śūlinī || 3 ||

vaktavyaṃ sādhakendreṇa trailokyānandadāyinī |
rakṣa māṃ tripure devi rakṣa māṃ tripureśvarī || 4 ||

rakṣa māṃ rakṣa māṃ devi asmadīyamidaṃ vapuḥ |

oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hsphreṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ aiṃ samayini madirānandasundari sakalasurāsuravandite śrīṃ 
hrīṃ aiṃ bhujagabhūpālamaulimālārcitacaraṇakamale vikaṭadante jaṭāṭopanivāsini madīyaṃ śarīraṃ 
rakṣa rakṣa parameśvari huṃ phaṭ svāhā oṃ bhū svāhā oṃ bhuva svāhā (p. 98) oṃ sva svāhā oṃ 
bhurbhuvasvasvāhā narāntraṃ mālābharaṇavibhūṣite mahākaulini brahmavādini mahādhanonmādakāriṇi 
mahābhogaprade asmadīya śarīraṃ vajamayaṃ kuru kuru durjanān han han mahīpālān kṣobhaya 
kṣobhaya paracakraṃ bhañjaya bhañjaya jayaṅkari gamanagāmini trailokyasvāmini samalavarayarūṃ 
ramalavarayarūṃ yamalavarayarūṃ bhamalavarayarūṃ kṣamalavarayarūṃ śrībhairavi prasīda prasīda 
svāhā iti vyāpya 

eṣā vidyā mahāvidyā samayā balavattarā |
ativīryatarā tībrasūryakoṭisamaprabhā ||

kulāṅganā kulaṃ sarvaṃ madīyaṃ parameśvarī |
devi rakṣatu divyāṅgaṃ divyāṅgī bhogadāyinī ||

rakṣa māṃ rakṣa māṃ devi śarīraṃ parameśvarī |
madīyaṃ madirānandamāpādatalamastaka ||

nirālambe mahātattve līyamānā sureśvara |
huṃkāraṃ ca tataḥ kṛtvā pūjayed bhūmimaṇḍale ||

(p. 99) pūjānte vāmahastena hrīṃkāratrividhaṃ japet |
paścāt traipuramantrāṇāṃ mūlamantreṇa śaṅkara ||

mālāmantravidhānena krameṇoccāraṇaṃ bhavet |

aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ oṃ namastripurasundari hṛdayadevi śirodevītyādi mahāśrīcakranagarasāmrājñī namaste 
namaste svāhā ityantaśca iti śuddhaśaktimālāmantraḥ |

kāmarājabhuktvā aiṃ bhagavati trikoṇāya namaḥ 

tripurākhyā mahādevī bhuktimuktiphalapradā |
darśaneṣu samasteṣu pāṣāṇḍeṣu viśeṣataḥ ||

divyarūpā mahādevī sarvatra parameśvarī |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mantrarājamimaṃ param ||

aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ pīṭhopapīṭhamadhyesthā gaganagiribhuvanagokulanivāsini oṃ kulakaulavibhedini 
sakalajanamanānandakāriṇi karotu mama cintitakārya śatamekabhairavī punātu parameśvarī 
madanamaṇḍalanandini saptakoṭimantrāṇāṃ parameśvarī svāhā ||

(p. 100) tripuro'yaṃ mahāmantraḥ kathyate sarvayogibhiḥ |
mantrodya devadeveśī janānandasvarūpiṇī ||

vīraśāyanalokānāṃ sarvasiddhiphalapradā |
siddhi yogāśca yāni karmāṇi bhūtale ||

tāni sarvāṇi sidhyanti mantreṇānena śaṅkara |
anenāpi ca saṃvyāpya tato mantrāntaraṃ nyaset ||

aiṃ namo bhagavatī trikoṇe tridhāvarṇe mahāliṅgālaṅkṛte trailokyotpattisthiti pralayakāriṇi sahala hrīṃ 
kandarpānandadāyini ----------- brahmadaṇḍarekhe ------------- vidyutpadme ------------- pāśāṅkuśālaṅkṛte vada

sarvāṅge vyāpakaṃ nyaset |

oṃ aiṃ sarvabījamātaḥ śrīsamayini mama manorathaṃ dehi dehi svāhā śrīmantrarājāya namaḥ iti 
brahmarandhre | 
aiṃ shīṃ shīṃ sakalahrīṃ shrīṃ hsrauṃ tripurabhairavyai namaḥ iti - bhrūmadhye | 
aiṃ hrīṃ sauḥ aiṃ kroṃ tripurāyai namaḥ - hṛdaye | 
hsrauṃ klīṃ mohinyai namaḥ - nābhau | 
aiṃ klīṃ blūṃ saḥ bhrāmarī tripurāyai namaḥ - liṅge | 
hsrauṃ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ preṃ trailokyasvāminyai namaḥ - mūlādhāre | 
aiṃ klīṃ sauḥ caṇḍikāyai namaḥ - kaṭyoḥ |
aiṃ aiṃ sauḥ kapālāṃkuravāsinyai tripurāyai namaḥ - pādayoḥ |

evaṃ vinyasya yathāśaktyā aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ haṃ saṃ iti japitvā aiṃ aiṃ sauḥ 

śvetabījānimānyevaṃ dhyānayukto vicintayet |
dhyānānte śrīmahārudra mantrī ślokaśataṃ japet ||

(p. 102) śaktirudramayaṃ dehaṃ madīyaṃ tripure kuru |
dehi me deva deveśi nitye nityamabhīpsitam || 1 ||

mastakaṃ maṅgalādevī lalāṭaṃ kulasundari |
netrayugmaṃ mahākālī karṇau rakṣatu kuṇḍalī || 2 ||

kapālī karṇarandhre ca kapolau kamalāvalī |
dantān rakṣatu cāmuṇḍā cubukaṃ meruvāsinī || 3 ||

bhrūmadhye kaṇṭhadeśaṃ tu rakṣanme bhuvaneśvarī |
jihvāṃ sarasvatī rakṣet taddukaṃ tāluvāsinī || 4 ||

sthātu me kamalā skandhe skandhā te kulamālinī |
kukṣau vināyakī sthā tu jayā nandāstanadvaye || 5 ||

kaṇṭhakūpe mahālakṣmī hṛdaye caṇḍabhairavi |
brahmāṇī nābhideśe tu sthātu jvālāvalī gude || 6 ||

liṅge liṅgaprabhā caiva muṇḍinī medamaṇḍale |
nāḍicakre mahāvegā udbhaṭā dakṣiṇe kare || 7 ||

(p. 103) vāmahaste mahāmāyā vidyā hastāṅgulīṣu ca |
vaiṣṇavī vāmapāde ca sthātu cakrāyudhānvitā || 8 ||

tathā dakṣiṇapāde tu ekapātparameśvarī |
pādāṅguliṣu kauberī romakūpe mahodbhavā || 9 ||

maṇḍalī nakhamūleṣu vārāhi meḍhramūlake |
jālandharī jalasthāne kāmākṣī kāmamadhyagā || 10 ||

udbhaṭānāmiliṅgānte nāsāgre pūrṇapīṭhagā |
pṛṣṭhavaṃśe jayā devī asthisandhiṣu carcikā || 11 ||

carmadhārī tvacā meḍhraṃ sthātu nityaṃ mahāyaśā |
raktamadhye namontā ca sthātu me haṃsinī sadā || 12 ||

māheśvarī kumārī dvau mamaiṃ te sthātu cāṅgake |
vāmadakṣiṇayoścaiva dhīrākṣī kaṭisandhiṣu || 13 ||

devī rakṣatu me gātraṃ mastakaṃ kulagāminī |
pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca || 14 ||

(p. 104) pañcabhūtātmabhūteśī sadā rakṣatu me kulam |
rājyaṃ dadātu me caindrī prajāṃ caiva prajāpati || 15 ||

māyā dadātu me nityaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ yaśastathā |
raṇe rājakule caiva śatrumadhye mahāvane || 16 ||

raktanetra mahādevī karotu mama cintitam |
samayaṃ samayā rakṣet vidyāṃ vidyā kulāgame || 17 ||

sādhakānāṃ jagannātha bhuktimuktiphalapradā |
pūrṇākarotu me siddhiṃ trailokyapi jayā sukham || 18 ||

ghaṇṭālī ca mahāvidyā sā me rakṣatu ghaṇṭikām |
saptakoṭisahasrāṇāṃ mantrāṇāṃ nāyikā tu yā || 19 ||

sā me sureśvarī devī sadā siddhiṃ prayacchatu |
ulkāmukhī mukhe sthātu mārjāri dehasandhiṣu || 20 ||

bhadrakālī tu yā vidyā sā me sthātu śivālaye |
trikoṇaṃ ca tridhā vṛttaṃ traipuraṃ cakramuttamam || 21 ||

(p. 105) mastake sthātu me nityaṃ tasyānte bahurūpiṇī |
pūrvoktā tripurāśakti sthātu me manmathotthitā || 22 ||

kṣobhayanti jagatsarvaṃ madirānandavihvalā |
nivāsaṃ kuru me dehe sāmprataṃ divyayoginī || 23 ||

ehyehi tvaṃ mahādevī siddhayogini me kulam |
śatrūṇāṃ ghātanārthāya jantūnāṃ bhogadāyinī || 24 ||

mahāyogini dehesmin sarvadā nilayaṃ kuru |
māhendrī me śikhāyāṃ ca yonimadhye gaṇeśvarī || 25 ||

pretākṣī nāma vikhyātā karotu kuśalaṃ mama |
ḍākinī pūrvabhāge ca mama saukhyaṃ prayacchatu || 26 ||

sākinī paścime bhāge dakṣiṇe cāpi rākinī |
vāmabhāge mahāmāyā karotu kuśalaṃ mama || 27 ||

sāsmadīyaṃ śirasthātu sadā rakṣatu bhairavī |
sthātu bhāle viśālākṣī nirmalāmalavarjitā || 28 ||

(p. 106) sā yoginī mahāyogā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
yā kālakalpitā kālī kālarātrīti kathyate || 29 ||

sā yoginī mahāyogā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
niśācarā ca rāja pūjitā ca niśācarī || 30 ||

sā yoginī mahāyogā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
sundarī cordhvakeśī ca kopanā muktakeśikā || 31 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
yā vīreti samākhyātā vīrāṇāṃ jayadāyinī || 32 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
yā mālinī samākhyātā nāsāgre bindumālinī || 33 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
yā kaṅkāla karālāṅgī candrakaṅkālakuṇḍalā || 34 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
pracaṇḍā ca virūpākṣī virūpā viśvarūpiṇī || 35 ||

(p. 107) sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
khaṭvāṅgī kathyate yā ca raudrī rudreṇa pūjitā || 36 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
kulayogini prasiddhā ca yā loke śrūyate kalau || 37 ||

sā yogini mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
pretākṣī kathyate yā ca phūtkārotkaragarjitā || 38 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
dhūmrākṣī yā samākhyātā śāstresmin yoginīmate || 39 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
ghorarūpā mahādevī kathyate yā kulāgame || 40 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
viśvarūpā viśeṣeṇa karoti ca gatryam || 41 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
bhayaṃ kṣayi mahādevi yā coktā vaiṣṇavāgame || 42 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
(p. 108) kapālamālikā yuktā yā devī muṇḍadhāriṇī || 43 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
bhīṣaṇā bhairavī nāma yā devī bhimavikramā || 44 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
nyagrodhavāsinī yā ca kathyate ca surārcitā || 45 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
bhīṣaṇā bhairavī yā ca bhairavāṣṭakavanditā || 46 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
pretākṣī dīrghalamboṣṭhī mahāmāyā mahābalā || 47 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
khaṭvāṅgī yā mahāśaktiḥ saṃsārārṇava tāriṇī || 48 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
yā samasteṣu mantreṣu procyate mantravādini || 49 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
(p. 109) kālaghnī kathyate yā ca yugānte parameśvarī || 50 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
gṛhiṇīti samākhyātā surāsura mahoragaiḥ || 51 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthārtu śrīrmastake mama |
cakriṇī gadyate yā ca ekapādāttrilocanā || 52 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
yā viśvabāhukā devī viśvanāthapriyā satī || 53 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
darśaneṣu samasteṣu vanditā bhuvaneśvarī || 54 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
kaṇṭakaddedanārthāya śāstre yā kaṇṭakī smṛtā || 55 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
kīlakī kathyate yā ca saptahastā mahābalā || 56 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
saṃgrāme yā mahādevī mahāmārīti kathyate || 57 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
yamadaṃṣṭreti vikhyātā surāsurasupūjitā || 58 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
karālinī tu yā devī mahāmāyā mahābalā || 59 ||

sā yoginī mahāmāyā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
lalitāmbā mahādevī sarvacakraikanāyakā || 60 ||

sā yoginī mahāyogā sthātu śrīrmastake mama |
nāsāgre kaulinī sthātu madanākṣī tathā mukhe || 61 ||

romakūpe kapole tu tāluke cāpadhāriṇī |
drāviṇī kṣobhiṇī caiva stambhinī mohinī tathā || 62 ||

raudrakarma mahāghaṇṭā ḍāmarī tvaritā matiḥ |
aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ kulamātā ca kākadṛṣṭiradhomukhī || 63 ||

kapālakuṇḍalā caiva kapālī kulamālinī |
(p. 111) devī rakṣatu me gātraṃ mastakaṃ kulamālinī || 64c

bhūmirāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca |
pañcabhūteṣu bhūteśī sadā rakṣatu me kulam || 65 ||

rājyaṃ dadātu me caindrīṃ prajāṃ caiva prajāvatī |
māyā dadātu me nityaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ mahadyaśaḥ || 66 ||

raṇe rājakule vāpi śatrumadhye mahājale |
raktanetrā mahādevī karotu mama cintitam || 67 ||

samayaṃ samayā rakṣed vidyāṃ vidyā kulāgame |
sādhakānāṃ jagannātha sā me siddhiphalapradā || 68 ||

dvijaṭī trijaṭī caiva kandalī lalitākhilā |
gāyatrī cāmbikā tārā pārvatī kamalā prabhā || 69 ||

modinī madanonmādā sundarī madanāturā |
viṣaṇā bhīṣaṇā nāma pretasiddhavibhīṣaṇī || 70 ||

kṣudhā tṛṣā tathā nidrā kānti vṛddhistathā dyutiḥ |
(p. 112) sandhyā rati kṣāntistvaniśaṃ paripaṭhyate || 71 ||

suranātheti vikhyātā nagare nagadevatā |
grāmadevī hyadhiṣṭhātrī pīṭhe pīṭheśvarī viduḥ || 72 ||

kāverī narmadā caiva gaṅgeti yamuneti ca |
godāvarī mahāpuṇyā procyate cāpyarundhatī || 73 ||

trailokye'pi mahādevī strīnāmnī yā prakīrtitā |
sā devītyupalakṣeta sthātu śrīrmastake mama || 74 ||

suvarṇalekhinī proktā vidyā sā procyate kila |
nirmūlini bhujaṅgānāṃ sā karotu sukhaṃ mama || 75 ||

kurukulleti vikhyātā pakṣirājakulodbhavā |
yā vidyā sā mahārūpā jihvāgre sthātu sarvadā || 76 ||

oṃkāriṇīti vikhyātā dehe sthātu sadā mama |
vighnāpahāriṇī vidyā kalirūpa vidāriṇī || 77 ||

bheruṇḍā pātu me kaṇṭhe totalā sthātu mastake |
tathā subalarekhāpi mūle sthātu sadā mama || 78 ||

jāṅgalī viṣanāśāya vācāsiddhiṃ karotu me |
sarvasiddhikarī vidyā bhuktimuktiphalapradā || 79 ||

lalāṭe maṅgalā sthātu vijayā sthātu mastake |
ekākṣī dakṣiṇaskandhe vāme caiva trilocanā || 80 ||

jayantī sthātu me kukṣau kaṭyāṃ kandarpamohinī |
mālinī liṅgasandhau ca hṛdi sthātu samādhinī || 81 ||

ambikā pṛṣṭhavaṃśe ca pārśvayo sthātu medinī |
diggajāṅgī karāgreṣu nāgendrī nakhasandhiṣu || 82 ||

vyāghrī cakrī ca jaṅghāyāṃ sthātu pādatale mahī |
amṛtā śaṅkhinī randhre locane ca vilāsinī || 83 ||

kālindī mūlajihvā ca raktaṃ rakṣatu raktinī |
lāṅgalī jāṅgulaṃ rakṣedasthinī cāsthisandhiṣu || 84 ||

majjinī dehamajjāṃ ca śukraṃ śukreśvarī tathā |
(p. 114) tvacaṃ rakṣatu vetālī mama rogapranāśinī || 85 ||

udbhaṭā kurutāṃ śāntiṃ sadaiva mama vigrahe |
padmāpādatale sthātu pathi rakṣatu panthinī || 86 ||

corāgnirājasarpebhyo bhayād rakṣatu bhairavī |
duṣṭānāṃ dṛṣṭibandhaṃ ca sadā karotu bandhinī || 87 ||

capeṭā nāma yā vidyā sā me karotu maṅgalām |
markaṭī ghaṭakarṇā ca hanumantī ca rāvaṇī || 88 ||

ghurghurīti ca vikhyātā vande vidyācatuṣṭayam |
ceṭakā jñānadā vidyā kaumārī caraṇāvalī || 89 ||

vighnarājaiḥ sutā nāma tuṣṭā santānarūpiṇī |
mūlākāśasthitā haṃsī pātakīdalanoddhṛtā || 90 ||

daśaitā mantravidyāstu tiṣṭhantu mama mastake |
śubhā me cāgratasthātu yoginī sthātu dakṣiṇe || 91 ||

vāmāṅge ratikāle ca paścame sthātu śṛ�nkhalā |
(p. 115) śikhā me śaṅkhinī rayodastaṃ vastravatī śubhā || 92 (93)||

kavace kavacāṅgī ca netre netrakṛtotsavā |
prakaṭā gopitāḥ sarvā bhīṣaṇā bhairavī śivā || 93 (94)||

saṃbhramā jayinī hamsā subhagā jaladevatā |
yakṣiṇī cūḍakanyā ca tathā cākāśagāminī || 94 ||

bhūcarī carakumbhī ca sakalāgamadevatā |
raṅgiṇī deva maitrī ca prasiddhā madirālikā || 95 ||

pūjitā sarvasidhyarthaṃ śāstre'smin yoginīmate |
sthāyinī gadyate sadbhiḥ sānandāvanavāsinī || 96 ||

catuṣaṣṭhyāśrayā devī trivargārthapradāyinī | 
namostu te jagannāthe namostu bhuvaneśvarī || 97 ||

namo bhogaprade devi namo devi maheśvarī |
namaḥ saṃhārapārasthe namo nirvāṇadāyinī || 98 ||

namostu te mahādevī namaḥ saṃsāratāriṇī |
(p. 116) ityetāstu mahāśambho yoginyo yena cintitāḥ || 99 ||

ādhāre līyamānastu sa yogī yogavid bhavet |
tiṣṭhanti yoginīrūpāḥ trailokya sacarācare || 100 (101)||

yoginyo yāstu tāḥ sarvā dṛḍhaṃ kurvatu me vapuḥ |
ahaṃ brahmādyahaṃ viṣṇurahameva maheśvaraḥ || 101 ||

sarvabhūtanivāsohaṃ loke śaktivicintakaḥ |
śaktinyāsakṛtenātra śarīreṇa surāsurāḥ || 102 ||

pradiddhoddeśamātreṇa ājñāṃ kurvatu me sadā |
yatkiñcid yoginīrūpaṃ trailokye cāsti śaṅkara || 103 ||

tatsarvaṃ tiṣṭhate dehe śaktinyāse kṛte sati |
kāminī kurute vāpi yaṃ nyāsaṃ śaktinirmitam || 104 ||

tāṃ devīṃ divyarūpasthāṃ saṃsāra tripurāṃ viduḥ |
putrāṇāṃ ca tathā deyaṃ bhaktānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 105 ||

śaktinyāsamidaṃ deyaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit |
(p. 117) manuṣyāṇāṃ mahīloke cintitārthaphalapradam || 106 ||

yaḥ karoti mama nyāsaṃ ṣoḍhānyāsādhikaṃ vibho |
sa jīvet śaktirūpeṇa trailokyonmūlana kṣama || 107 ||

śaktinyāsakṛte jīve yaḥ kaścit kṣobhako bhavet |
karmaṇā manasā vācā tasya mūrdhā patiṣyati || 108 ||

evaṃ śaktinyāsaṃ vidhāya mahācakreśvarīnyāsaṃ kuryāt |

pūrvavat hṛdikaraṣaḍaṅgadhyānapūrvaṃ yathā śaktiṃ japitvā japaṃ pūrvavatsamarpayet |

viniyogaḥ |

6 śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ tapolokanilayaśatakoṭi-atiguptahākinīyoginī mūladevatāyutādhāraśaktyambādevyai 
namaḥ - bhāle, 
6 laṃ kṣaṃ satyalokanilayaśatakoṭimahāguptayākinīyoginī mūladevatāyutādhāraśaktyambādevyai namaḥ - 
brahmarandhre | 
hsauṃḥ shauṃḥ akārādikṣāntaṃ mātṛkāmuccārya caturdaśabhuvanādhipāyai śrīparāmbādevyai 
namaḥ vyāpakaṃ kuryāt | iti bhuvananyāsaḥ |

atha mantranyāsaḥ -

6 kaṃ khaṃ gaṃ ṣaṭlakṣakoṭibhedaskandādiṣaḍākṣarātmākhilamantrādhidevatāyai sakalaphalapradāyai

6 ghaṃ ṅaṃ caṃ saptalakṣakoṭibhedagaṇeśādisaptākṣarātmākhilamantrādhidevatāyai sakalaphalapradāyai

6 ḍaṃ ḍhaṃ ṇaṃ daśalakṣakoṭibhedaviṣṇvādidaśākṣarātmākhilamantrādhidevatāyai sakalaphalapradāyai

(p. 127) 6 dhaṃ naṃ paṃ dvādaśalakṣakoṭibhedavāṇyādidvādaśākṣarātmākhilamantrādhidevatāyai

6 phaṃ baṃ bhaṃ trayodaśalakṣakoṭibhedalakṣmyāditrayodaśākṣarātmākhilamantrādhidevatāyai

6 maṃ yaṃ raṃ caturdaśalakṣakoṭibhedagauryādicaturdaśākṣarātmākhilamantradevatāyai

6 laṃ vaṃ śaṃ pañcadaśalakṣakoṭibhedadurgādipañcadaśākṣarātmākhilamantrādhi-devatāyai

ṣoḍaśakūṭeśvaryambādevyai namaḥ - śivasthāne, 
dhruvamaṇḍale bindvādīnāṃ daśacakrāṇāṃ sthānāni ājñācakrādūrdhvaṃ ekaikāṅgulamānena 
uparyupari tiṣṭhanti | dhruvamaṇḍale brahmarandhrāvasānabhūmiḥ sadāśivasthānaṃ mahābindurityarthaḥ | 
tato hsauṃḥ shauṃḥ aṃ āṃ ityādikṣāntaṃ sarvamantrādhipāyai parāmbādevyai namaḥ vyāpakaṃ 
kuryāt | iti mantranyāsaḥ |

atha mātaranyāsaḥ -

(p. 130) oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hsauṃḥ shauṃḥ kaṃ khaṃ gaṃ ghaṃ ṅaṃ anantakoṭibhūcarīkulasevitāyai 
āṃ āṃ maṅgalāmbādevyai āṃ śāṃ brahmāṇyambādevyai anantakoṭibhūtakulasahitāyai aṃ kṣaṃ 
maṅgalanāthāya aṃ kṣaṃ asitāṅgabhairavanāthāya namaḥ - mūlādhāre, 

6 caṃ chaṃ jaṃ jhaṃ ñaṃ anantakoṭikhecarīkulasahitāyai īṃ lāṃ carcikāmbādevyai īṃ lāṃ 
māheśvaryambādevyai anantakoṭivetālakulasahitāya īṃ laṃ carcikānāthāya iṃ laṃ rurubhairavanāthāya 
namaḥ - svādhiṣṭhāne, 

6 ṭaṃ ṭhaṃ ḍaṃ ḍhaṃ ṇaṃ anantakoṭi ------------ kulasahitāyai uṃ hāṃ kaumāryambādevyai 
anantakoṭipiśācakulasahitāya uṃ haṃ yogīśvaranāthāya uṃ haṃ caṇḍabhairavanāthāya namaḥ - 
maṇipūre,

6 taṃ thaṃ daṃ dhaṃ naṃ anantakoṭidikcarīkulasahitāyai ṝṃ sāṃ harasidhyambādevyai ṝṃ sāṃ vai 
----- vyambādevyai anantakoṭi-apasmārakulasahitāya ṛṃ saṃ harasiddhanāthāya ṛṃ saṃ 
krodhabhairavanāthāya namaḥ - anāhate,

6 paṃ phaṃ baṃ bhaṃ maṃ anantakoṭisahacarīkulasahitāyai ḹṃ ṣāṃ bhavyambādevyai ḷṃ ṣāṃ 
vārāhyambādevyai anantakoṭibrahmarākṣakulasahitāya ḷṃ ṣaṃ bhaṭṭanāthāya ḷṃ ṣaṃ 
unmattabhairavanāthāya namaḥ - viśuddhau, 

6 yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ anantakoṭigiricarīkulasahitāyai aiṃ śāṃ kilakilāmbādevyai aiṃ śāṃ 
indrāṇyambādevyai anantakoṭiceṭakakulasahitāya eṃ śaṃ kilikilānāthāya eṃ śaṃ 
kapālībhairavanāthāya namaḥ - ājñāyām, 

(p. 131) 6 śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ anantakoṭivanacarīkulasahitāyai auṃ vāṃ kālarātryambādevyai auṃ 
vāṃ cāmuṇḍādevyai anantakoṭipretakulasahitāya oṃ vaṃ kālarātrināthāya oṃ vaṃ 
bhīṣaṇabhairavanāthāya namaḥ - bhāle, 

6 laṃ kṣaṃ anantakoṭijalacarīkulasahitāyai aḥ lāṃ bhīṣaṇāmbādevyai aḥ lāṃ mahālakṣmyambādevyai 
anantakoṭikūṣmāṇḍakulasahitāya aṃ laṃ bhīṣaṇanāthāya aṃ laṃ saṃhārabhairavanāthāya namaḥ - 
brahmarandhre, 

6 aṃ āṃ ityādikṣāntaṃ samastamātṛbhairavādhipāyai parāmbādevyai namaḥ vyāpakaṃ kuryāt | iti 
mātṛkānyāsaḥ |

	tato hsāṃ shauṃ hsīṃ shīṃ ṣaṭdīrghabījena ṣaḍaṅgaṃ vidhāya devaṃ dhyāyet 

dhyāna mahākulaprakāśatantre -

evaṃ nyasta tanurdevī dhyāyed devamananyadhīḥ |
amṛtārṇavamadhyastha ratnadīpe manorame ||

kalpavṛkṣavanāntasthe navamāṇikyamaṇḍape |
navaratnamayaṃ śrīmat siṃhāsanāgatāmbuje ||

(p. 132) trikoṇāntaḥ samāsīnaṃ candrasūryayutaṃ prabham |
ardhāmbikāsamāyuktaṃ pravibhaktavibhūṣaṇam ||

koṭikandarpalāvaṇyaṃ sadā ṣoḍaśavārṣikam |
mandasmitamukhāmbhojaṃ trinetraṃ candraśekharam ||

divyāmbarasragālepa divyābharaṇabhūṣitam |
pānapātraṃ ca cinmudrāṃ triśūlaṃ pustakaṃ karaiḥ ||

vidyā saṃ sadi bibhrāṇaṃ sadānandamukhekṣaṇam |
mahāṣoḍhoditāśeṣadevatāgaṇasevitam ||

evaṃ cittāmbuje dhyāyedardhanārīśvaraṃ śivam |
puruṣaṃ vā smared devīṃ strīrūpaṃ vā vicintayet ||

athavā niṣkalaṃ dhyāyet saccidānandavigraham |
sarvatejomayaṃ divyaṃ sacarācaravigraham ||

tataḥ sandarśayet mudrādaśakaṃ parameśvarī |
yoniliṅgaṃ ca surabhiṃ heti mudrācatuṣṭayam ||

(p. 133) vanamālāṃ mahāmudrāṃ nabhomudrāmapi kramāt |
yathāśaktijapet mūlamantraṃ śrīpādukāmapi ||

	hetimudrā kapālajñānatriśūlaṃ pustakamiti | iti mudrāḥ pradarśa prāsāda 
parāparāprāsādātmakaṃ mūlamantraṃ śrīgurupādukāṃ ca yathā śaktijapitvā śirasi śrīguruṃ smaret 
| uktaṃ ca tatraiva -

mūrdhni saṃcintayed devī śrīguruṃ śivarūpiṇam |
sahasradalapaṅkaje sakalaśītaraśmiprabham ||

varābhayakarāmbujaṃ vimalagandhapuṣpāmbaram |
prasannavadanekṣaṇaṃ sakaladevatārūpiṇam ||

smarecchirasi --------- tadabhidhānapūrvaṃ gurum |

iti dhyātvā praṇamya svagurukṛtasvanāma mūlādhāre smṛtvā śivarūpaṃ svātmānaṃ vicintayet |

evaṃ nyāsakartuḥ phalamāha -

(p. 134) evaṃ nyāse kṛte devī sākṣāt paraśivo bhavet |
mantrī na cātra sandeho nigrahānugrahe kṣamaḥ ||

mahāṣoḍhāhvayaṃ nyāsaṃ yaḥ karoti hi pārvatī |
divyakṣetraṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ samantād daśayojanam ||

kṛtvā nyāsamimaṃ devī yatra gacchati mānavaḥ |
tatra syād dvijayo lābhaḥ sammānaṃ pauruṣaṃ priye ||

mahāṣoḍhākṛtanyāsastadajñaṃ yadi bandate |
māsānmṛtyumavāpnoti yadi trātā śiva svayam ||

vajrapañjaranāmānamevaṃ nyāsaṃ karoti yaḥ |
divyantarikṣabhūśailajalāranyanivāsinaḥ ||

uddaṇḍabhūtavetāla devarakṣograhādayaḥ |
bhayatrastena manasā nekṣante taṃ kuleśvarī ||

mahāṣoḍhāhvayaṃ nyāsaṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ |
devāḥ sarve'pi kurvanti ṛṣīyogīmunīśvarāḥ ||

(p. 135) bahunoktena kiṃ devī nyāsametat mama priyam |
nāputrāya vaded devī nāśiṣyāya prakāśayet ||

ājñāsiddhimavāpnoti rahasi nyāsamācaret |
asmātparatarā rakṣā devatābhāvasiddhidā ||

loke nāsti na sandeho satyaṃ satyaṃ varānane |

ayaṃ nyāsastvanekajanmārjitapuṇyai prāpyata ityata āha -

ūrdhvāmnāya praveśaśca parāprāsādacintanam |
mahāṣoḍhāparijñānaṃ nālpasya tapasaḥ phalam ||

iti mahāṣoḍhānyāsaḥ samāptaḥ | śrīsarasvatīmarpaṇamastu |

	asya śrīmahāpādukāmantrasya haṃsaṛṣiḥ avyaktagāyatrīchandaḥ śrīmahāpādukādevatā 
haṃsabījaṃ saḥ śaktiḥ so'haṃ kīlakaṃ mama mahāpādukādevatāprasādasidhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ |

	ṛṣyādinyāsaṃ vidhāya hsāmityādikaraṣaḍaṅganyāsaḥ | dhyānam -

(p. 136) sahasradalapaṅkaja

brahmānandaṃ paramasukhadaṃ

svaprakāśaśivamūrti

saktintayāṇi ca

iti dhyātvā abhayavaradakiriṭamakarakuṇḍalamudrāḥ pradarśa pañcopacāraiḥ sampūjya

iti kulārṇavavacanāt | ukta pañcadaśamantraiḥ sakṛtsakṛdabhimantrayet |

mantrāstu - ṛgvede yathā -

svā di ṣṭa yā ma di ṣṭa yā pa va sva so ma dhā ra yā i ndrā ya pā ta ve su taḥ |
(p. 141) ra kṣo hā vi śva ca rṣa ṇi ra bhi yo ni ma yo ha taṃ 
dru ṇā sa dha ---- mā sa da t |
va ri vo dhā tta mo bha va maṃ hi ṣṭo vṛ tra haṃ ta maḥ pa rṣi rā dho ma dho nāṃ |

a bhya rṣa ma hā nāṃ de vā nāṃ vī ti maṃ dha sā | a bhi vā ja mu ta śra vaḥ |

tvā ma cha ca rā ma si ta di da rthaṃ di ve di ve | i ndo tve na ā śa saḥ |

vi ṣṇu ryo niṃ ka lpa ya tu tva ṣṭā rū pā ṇi pi śa tu |

ā siṃ ca tu pra jā pa ti rdhā tā ga rbhaṃ da dhā tu te |

ga rbhaṃ dhe hi si nī vā li ga rbhaṃ dhe hi sa ra sva tī |

ga rbhaṃ te a śvi nau de vā vā dha ttāṃ pu ṣka ra sra jā |

hi ra ṇya yī a ra ṇī ya ni rma tha to a śvi nā |

taṃ te ga rbhaṃ ha vā ma he da śa me mā si sū ta ve |

tri bhi ṣṭaṃ va de va sa vi ta rva rṣi ṣṭhaiḥ so ma dhā ma bhiḥ a gne da kṣaiḥ pu nī hi naḥ pu 
naṃ tu māṃ de va ja nāḥ pu naṃ tu va sa vo dhi yā |

(p 142) vi śve de vāḥ pu nī ta mā jā ta ve daḥ pu nī hi mā |

pra pyā ya sva pra sthaṃ da sva so ma vi śve bhi ra śu bhiḥ de ve bhya u tta maṃ ha viḥ | 

i maṃ me gaṃ ge ya mu ne sa ra sva tī śu tu dri sto maṃ sa ca tā pa ru ---- |

a si kryā ma ru dvṛ dhe vi ta sta yā ----- ye śṛ ṇu --- su ṣo ma yā |

si tā si te sa ri te ya tra saṃ ga te ta trā plu tā so di va mu tpa ttaṃ ti |

e vai ta nvāṃ vi sṛ ja nti dhi rā ste na nā so a mṛ ta tvaṃ bha jaṃ te |

tā maṃ da sā nā ma nu ṣo du ro ṇa ā dha ttaṃ ra yiṃ sa ha vī raṃ va ca sya ve |

kṛ taṃ tī rthaṃ su pra pā ṇaṃ śu bha sya tī sthā ṇu ya the ṣṭhā ma pa du rma tiṃ ha taṃ |

śro ṇā me ka mu da kaṃ gā ma vā ja ti māṃ sa me kaḥ piṃ śa ti sū na yā bhṛ taṃ |

ā ni gṛ caḥ śa kṛ de ko a pā bha ra ṇi *? *? bhyaḥ pi va śa *? pā va tuḥ |

baindave parameśāni madhyasiṃhāsane tathā |

śrīvidyā tvaritā caiva pārijāteśvarī tathā ||

pañcaratneśvarī tripurasundarī śrī0 |
